      "text": "Of course. It\u0027s a great idea to break these key topics out into their own focused notes. This will make them easier to study and link to in your Obsidian vault.\n\nI\u0027ve created three separate, detailed notes based on your presentation slides and handwritten notes, focusing on the derivations and explanations you requested.\n\n---\n\n### File 1: `Fizika_I_Neenakomerno_Gibanje.md`\n\n# [[Neenakomerno Pospešeno Premo Gibanje]]\n\nThis note covers motion where the acceleration is not constant but changes over time. This is a more general and powerful way to describe motion than the simple, uniformly accelerated case.\n\n### [[Definicija (Definition)]]\nIn non-uniformly accelerated motion, the acceleration is a function of time:\n$$ a \u003d a(t) $$\n**Ključna Razlika (The Key Difference):**\nBecause acceleration is no longer a constant number, the simple algebraic equations like $v \u003d v\u0027 + at$ **no longer apply**. We must use calculus (integration and differentiation) to move between position, velocity, and acceleration.\n\n### [[Temeljne Povezave (Fundamental Relationships)]]\n\nThe relationships are based on the definitions of instantaneous velocity and acceleration as derivatives:\n\n| Za določitev... (To find...) | Iz... (From...) | Operacija (Operation)                                                               | Formula                                                                  |\n| :-------------------------- | :-------------- | :---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| **Pospešek (Acceleration)** | Hitrost (Velocity) | **Odvod (Derivative)**                                                              | $a(t) \u003d \\frac{dv}{dt} \u003d \\dot{v}$                                         |\n| **Hitrost (Velocity)**      | Lega (Position)   | **Odvod (Derivative)**                                                              | $v(t) \u003d \\frac{dx}{dt} \u003d \\dot{x}$                                         |\n| **Hitrost (Velocity)**      | Pospešek (Acceleration) | **Integral**                                                                        | $v(t) \u003d v(t\u0027) + \\int_{t\u0027}^{t} a(\\tau) \\, d\\tau$                           |\n| **Lega (Position)**         | Hitrost (Velocity)   | **Integral**                                                                        | $x(t) \u003d x(t\u0027) + \\int_{t\u0027}^{t} v(\\tau) \\, d\\tau$                           |\n\n\u003e **Opomba (Note):** The relationship $a \u003d \\frac{d^2x}{dt^2} \u003d \\ddot{x}$ also holds, connecting acceleration directly to the second derivative of position.\n\n### [[Primer: Gibanje Lokomotive (Locomotive Motion Example)]]\n\nThis was assigned as a \"DN\" (homework) problem in your notes. Let\u0027s solve it step-by-step.\n\n**Naloga (Problem):**\nHitrost lokomotive narašča kot $v(t) \u003d k\\sqrt{t}$, kjer je $k$ konstanta. Določi časovno odvisnost pospeška in lege lokomotive. (The velocity of a locomotive increases as $v(t) \u003d k\\sqrt{t}$, where $k$ is a constant. Determine the time dependence of the acceleration and the position.)\n\n**1. Iskanje Pospeška (Finding Acceleration)**\n\n*   **Strategija:** Pospešek je prvi odvod hitrosti po času.\n    $$ a(t) \u003d \\frac{dv}{dt} $$\n*   **Izračun:**\n    $$ a(t) \u003d \\frac{d}{dt} \\left( k \\sqrt{t} \\right) \u003d \\frac{d}{dt} \\left( k t^{1/2} \\right) $$\n    Using the power rule for differentiation $(\\frac{d}{dt}t^n \u003d nt^{n-1})$:\n    $$ a(t) \u003d k \\cdot \\left( \\frac{1}{2} t^{\\frac{1}{2}-1} \\right) \u003d k \\cdot \\frac{1}{2} t^{-1/2} $$\n*   **Rešitev:**\n    $$ a(t) \u003d \\frac{k}{2\\sqrt{t}} $$\n    *   **Analiza:** The acceleration is not constant; it decreases as time goes on. The initial acceleration at $t \\to 0$ is infinite, which is a physical idealization.\n\n**2. Iskanje Lege (Finding Position)**\n\n*   **Strategija:** Lega je integral hitrosti po času. Predpostavimo, da lokomotiva štarta iz izhodišča ob času $t\u003d0$, torej $x(0) \u003d 0$.\n    $$ x(t) \u003d \\int_{0}^{t} v(\\tau) \\, d\\tau $$\n*   **Izračun:**\n    $$ x(t) \u003d \\int_{0}^{t} k\\sqrt{\\tau} \\, d\\tau \u003d k \\int_{0}^{t} \\tau^{1/2} \\, d\\tau $$\n    Using the power rule for integration $(\\int \\tau^n d\\tau \u003d \\frac{\\tau^{n+1}}{n+1})$:\n    $$ x(t) \u003d k \\left[ \\frac{\\tau^{\\frac{1}{2}+1}}{\\frac{1}{2}+1} \\right]_0^t \u003d k \\left[ \\frac{\\tau^{3/2}}{3/2} \\right]_0^t \u003d \\frac{2k}{3} [\\tau^{3/2}]_0^t $$\n*   **Rešitev:**\n    $$ x(t) \u003d \\frac{2k}{3} t^{3/2} $$\n\n---\n\n### File 2: `Fizika_I_Vektorji.md`\n\n# [[Vektorji (Vectors)]]\n\nVektor je količina, ki je določena z **velikostjo (magnitude)** in **smerjo (direction)** v prostoru.\n\n### [[Osnovne Operacije (Basic Operations)]]\n*   **Množenje s skalarjem (Scalar Multiplication):** Množenje vektorja $\\vec{a}$ s skalarjem (številom) spremeni njegovo dolžino. Če je skalar negativen, se obrne tudi smer.\n*   **Seštevanje in Odštevanje (Addition and Subtraction):** Vektorje seštevamo grafično po **pravilu trikotnika (head-to-tail)** ali **paralelograma**. Odštevanje $\\vec{a} - \\vec{b}$ je definirano kot seštevanje z nasprotnim vektorjem: $\\vec{a} + (-\\vec{b})$.\n\n### [[Množenje Vektorjev (Multiplication of Vectors)]]\nObstajata dva glavna načina množenja vektorjev.\n\n#### [[Skalarni Produkt (Scalar or \"Dot\" Product)]]\nRezultat skalarnega produkta je **skalar (številka)**.\n$$ \\vec{a} \\cdot \\vec{b} \u003d |\\vec{a}| |\\vec{b}| \\cos\\varphi $$\n*   **Pomen (The \"Why\"):** Skalarni produkt meri \"koliko enega vektorja leži v smeri drugega\". Je projekcija vektorja $\\vec{b}$ na smer vektorja $\\vec{a}$, pomnožena z dolžino $\\vec{a}$.\n*   **Lastnost:** Če sta vektorja pravokotna ($\\varphi \u003d 90^\\circ$), je njun skalarni produkt enak **nič**, ker $\\cos(90^\\circ)\u003d0$.\n\n#### [[Vektorski Produkt (Vector or \"Cross\" Product)]]\nRezultat vektorskega produkta je **nov vektor**.\n$$ \\vec{c} \u003d \\vec{a} \\times \\vec{b} $$\n*   **Velikost (Magnitude):** Velikost novega vektorja je enaka ploščini paralelograma, ki ga napenjata vektorja $\\vec{a}$ in $\\vec{b}$.\n    $$ |\\vec{c}| \u003d |\\vec{a} \\times \\vec{b}| \u003d |\\vec{a}| |\\vec{b}| \\sin\\varphi $$\n*   **Smer (Direction):** Novi vektor $\\vec{c}$ je **pravokoten** na ravnino, ki jo določata $\\vec{a}$ in $\\vec{b}$. Njegova usmeritev je določena s **pravilom desne roke**: Če prste desne roke pokrčimo od prvega vektorja ($\\vec{a}$) proti drugemu ($\\vec{b}$), iztegnjeni palec pokaže smer rezultata $\\vec{c}$.\n*   **Lastnost:** Vrstni red je pomemben!\n    $$ \\vec{a} \\times \\vec{b} \u003d -(\\vec{b} \\times \\vec{a}) $$\n\n### [[Komponente Vektorjev (Vector Components)]]\nVektorje najlažje računsko obravnavamo z razstavljanjem na komponente v koordinatnem sistemu, ki ga določajo enotski vektorji $\\vec{i}, \\vec{j}, \\vec{k}$.\n$$ \\vec{a} \u003d a_x \\vec{i} + a_y \\vec{j} + a_z \\vec{k} \\quad \\text{ali} \\quad \\vec{a} \u003d (a_x, a_y, a_z) $$\n\n| Operacija                               | Formula v Komponentah                                                                                                                                              |\n| --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |\n| **Seštevanje (Addition)**               | $\\vec{a}+\\vec{b} \u003d (a_x+b_x, a_y+b_y, a_z+b_z)$                                                                                                                     |\n| **Skalarni Produkt (Dot Product)**      | $\\vec{a} \\cdot \\vec{b} \u003d a_x b_x + a_y b_y + a_z b_z$                                                                                                                |\n| **Velikost (Magnitude)**                | $|\\vec{a}| \u003d \\sqrt{\\vec{a} \\cdot \\vec{a}} \u003d \\sqrt{a_x^2 + a_y^2 + a_z^2}$                                                                                             |\n| **Vektorski Produkt (Cross Product)**   | $\\vec{a} \\times \\vec{b} \u003d (a_y b_z - a_z b_y, \\quad a_z b_x - a_x b_z, \\quad a_x b_y - a_y b_x)$                                                                     |\n\n---\n\n### File 3: `Fizika_I_Navpicni_Met.md`\n\n# [[Navpični met (Vertical Throw)]]\n\nNavpični met je klasičen primer **enakomerno pospešenega premočrtnega gibanja**, kjer je pospešek konstanten in posledica gravitacije.\n\n### [[Osnovni Model in Predpostavke]]\n1.  **Koordinatni Sistem:** Postavimo os $y$ navpično navzgor. Izhodišče ($y\u003d0$) je na mestu izmeta.\n2.  **Pospešek (Acceleration):** Težni pospešek $g$ vedno kaže navzdol. Ker je naša os usmerjena navzgor, je pospešek negativen:\n    $$ a_y \u003d -g \\approx -9.81 \\, \\frac{m}{s^2} $$\n3.  **Začetni Pogoji (Initial Conditions):** Ob času $t\u0027\u003d0$ je telo na $y\u0027\u003d0$ z neko začetno hitrostjo $v_y\u0027$.\n\n### [[Osnovne Enačbe Gibanja]]\nEnačbe za enakomerno pospešeno gibanje prilagodimo za navpično smer:\n*   **Hitrost:** $v_y(t) \u003d v_y\u0027 + a_y t \u003d v_y\u0027 - gt$\n*   **Lega:** $y(t) \u003d y\u0027 + v_y\u0027t + \\frac{1}{2}a_y t^2 \u003d v_y\u0027t - \\frac{1}{2}gt^2$\n\n### [[Izpeljava Ključnih Količin (Derivation of Key Quantities)]]\n\n#### [[Najvišja Višina (H) in Čas Dvigovanja ($t_H$)]]\n*   **Razlaga (The \"Why\"):** Na najvišji točki svoje poti se telo za trenutek ustavi in obrne smer. V tem trenutku je njegova trenutna hitrost enaka nič.\n    $$ v_y(t_H) \u003d 0 $$\n*   **Izpeljava časa $t_H$:** Uporabimo enačbo za hitrost in jo nastavimo na 0:\n    $$ 0 \u003d v_y\u0027 - g t_H $$\n    $$ g t_H \u003d v_y\u0027 \\implies \\boldsymbol{t_H \u003d \\frac{v_y\u0027}{g}} $$\n*   **Izpeljava višine $H$:** Višino $H$ doseže ob času $t_H$. Ta čas vstavimo v enačbo za lego:\n    $$ H \u003d y(t_H) \u003d v_y\u0027 (t_H) - \\frac{1}{2}g(t_H)^2 $$\n    $$ H \u003d v_y\u0027 \\left(\\frac{v_y\u0027}{g}\\right) - \\frac{1}{2}g\\left(\\frac{v_y\u0027}{g}\\right)^2 \u003d \\frac{v_y\u0027^2}{g} - \\frac{1}{2}g \\frac{v_y\u0027^2}{g^2} $$\n    $$ H \u003d \\frac{v_y\u0027^2}{g} - \\frac{v_y\u0027^2}{2g} \\implies \\boldsymbol{H \u003d \\frac{v_y\u0027^2}{2g}} $$\n\n#### [[Celotni Čas Leta ($t_0$)]]\n*   **Razlaga (The \"Why\"):** Celotni čas leta je čas, ki ga telo potrebuje, da se vrne na začetno višino ($y\u003d0$).\n    $$ y(t_0) \u003d 0 $$\n*   **Izpeljava:** Uporabimo enačbo za lego:\n    $$ 0 \u003d v_y\u0027 t_0 - \\frac{1}{2}g t_0^2 $$\n    To je kvadratna enačba za $t_0$. Izpostavimo $t_0$:\n    $$ t_0 \\left( v_y\u0027 - \\frac{1}{2}g t_0 \\right) \u003d 0 $$\n    Enačba ima dve rešitvi:\n    1.  $t_0 \u003d 0$ (Trivialna rešitev; telo je bilo na višini 0 ob začetku.)\n    2.  $v_y\u0027 - \\frac{1}{2}g t_0 \u003d 0 \\implies \\boldsymbol{t_0 \u003d \\frac{2v_y\u0027}{g}}$\n*   **Opazovanje:** $t_0 \u003d 2 \\cdot t_H$. Čas dviganja je enak času padanja nazaj na isto višino.\n\n### [[Prosti Pad (Free Fall)]]\nProsti pad je poseben primer navpičnega meta, kjer je začetna hitrost enaka nič ($v_y\u0027 \u003d 0$).",

      "text": "Of course. I\u0027ve processed your handwritten notes and the textbook for your \"Računalništvo v kemiji\" (Computing in Chemistry) course.\n\nThis is an excellent first lecture, covering the foundational concepts of algorithmic thinking and the number systems that computers use. I\u0027ve created a new, comprehensive note for your Obsidian vault. It explains the core concepts, breaks down the mathematical examples from your notes step-by-step with justifications, and structures everything logically.\n\n---\n\n### File: `Racunalnistvo_v_Kemiji_Uvod.md`\n\n# [[Uvod v Računalništvo v Kemiji]]\n\nThis lecture introduces the fundamental principles of computational thinking in a chemical and engineering context. The focus is on structuring problems logically through algorithms, understanding the number systems that computers use, and applying these concepts to a practical chemical problem.\n\n## [[Algoritemski Pristop k Reševanju Problemov]]\n\nThe core of programming isn\u0027t just writing code; it\u0027s about a systematic approach to solving problems. Your notes outline a clear four-step process.\n\n1.  **Definicija Problema (Problem Definition):** Clearly state what needs to be solved. What are the inputs? What are the desired outputs?\n2.  **Analiza Problema in Modeliranje (Problem Analysis and Modeling):** Gather all necessary equations, constants, and relationships. Ensure all units are consistent. This is the physics and chemistry foundation.\n3.  **Zasnova Algoritma / Psevdokoda (Algorithm Design / Pseudocode):** Create a step-by-step, human-readable plan to get from the inputs to the outputs.\n4.  **Programski Pristop (Programming Implementation):** Translate the algorithm into a specific programming language (like Python).\n\n### [[Diagram Poteka (Flowchart)]]\nA flowchart is a **vizualna predstavitev (visual representation)** of an algorithm. It uses standard symbols to map the logical flow of a program.\n\n| Simbol (Symbol) | Pomen (Meaning) | Primer Uporabe (Example of Use) |\n| :---: | :---: | :--- |\n| **Oval** | Začetek / Stop (Start / Stop) | Marks the beginning and end of the program. |\n| **Paralelogram** | Vhod / Izhod (Input / Output) | Reading data (e.g., mass, temp) or displaying results. |\n| **Pravokotnik** | Enačbe / Proces (Equations / Process) | Performing a calculation (e.g., `n \u003d m / M`). |\n| **Romb** | Pogoj / Odločitev (Condition / Decision) | A \"yes/no\" question that directs the program\u0027s flow (e.g., `if T \u003e 273`). |\n| **Šestkotnik** | Zanka (Loop) | A block of code that is repeated multiple times. |\n\n---\n\n## [[Številski Sistemi v Računalništvu]]\n\nComputers don\u0027t think in decimal (base-10); they operate in **dvojiški sistem (binary, base-2)** using on/off electrical states, represented by 1s and 0s. Understanding how to convert between these systems is fundamental.\n\n### [[Pretvarjanje: Desetiški v Dvojiški Sistem (Decimal to Binary)]]\nThe process is split into two parts: the integer part and the fractional part.\n\n#### **1. Celi Del (Integer Part): Metoda Zaporednega Deljenja**\nWe repeatedly divide the integer by 2 and record the remainders. The binary number is formed by reading the remainders from **bottom to top**.\n\n**Primer (Example):** Convert the integer `10` from the number $10.625_{10}$.\n\n| Deljenje (Division) | Rezultat (Result) | **Ostanek (Remainder)** |\n| :---: | :---: | :---: |\n| $10 \\div 2$ | 5 | **0** |\n| $5 \\div 2$ | 2 | **1** |\n| $2 \\div 2$ | 1 | **0** |\n| $1 \\div 2$ | 0 | **1** $\\uparrow$ (Read upwards) |\n\nTherefore, the integer part is $10_{10} \u003d 1010_2$.\n\n#### **2. Decimalni Del (Fractional Part): Metoda Zaporednega Množenja**\nWe repeatedly multiply the fractional part by 2 and record the integer part of the result. The binary fraction is formed by reading these integer parts from **top to bottom**.\n\n**Primer (Example):** Convert the fraction `0.625` from $10.625_{10}$.\n\n| Množenje (Multiplication) | Rezultat (Result) | **Celi del (Integer Part)** |\n| :---: | :---: | :---: |\n| $0.625 \\times 2$ | 1.25 | **1** $\\downarrow$ (Read downwards) |\n| $0.25 \\times 2$ | 0.50 | **0** |\n| $0.50 \\times 2$ | 1.00 | **1** |\n\nThe process stops when the fractional part becomes 0. The fractional part is $0.625_{10} \u003d 0.101_2$.\n\n**Končni Rezultat:** Combining both parts, we get:\n$$ 10.625_{10} \u003d 1010.101_2 $$\n\n### [[Pretvarjanje: Dvojiški v Desetiški Sistem (Binary to Decimal)]]\n**Razlaga (The \"Why\"):** This conversion is a weighted sum of powers of 2. Each position in the binary number corresponds to a power of 2. The powers are positive for the integer part (starting from 0 at the decimal point and increasing to the left) and negative for the fractional part (starting from -1 and decreasing to the right).\n\n**Primer (Example):** Convert $11001.0111_2$ to decimal.\n\n| Dvojiška Števka (Binary Digit) | Položaj ($i$) (Position) | Potenca ($2^i$) (Power) | Vrednost (Digit $\\times 2^i$) (Value) |\n| :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: |\n| 1 | 4 | $2^4 \u003d 16$ | 16 |\n| 1 | 3 | $2^3 \u003d 8$ | 8 |\n| 0 | 2 | $2^2 \u003d 4$ | 0 |\n| 0 | 1 | $2^1 \u003d 2$ | 0 |\n| 1 | 0 | $2^0 \u003d 1$ | 1 |\n| . | | (Decimal Point) | |\n| 0 | -1 | $2^{-1} \u003d 0.5$ | 0 |\n| 1 | -2 | $2^{-2} \u003d 0.25$ | 0.25 |\n| 1 | -3 | $2^{-3} \u003d 0.125$ | 0.125 |\n| 1 | -4 | $2^{-4} \u003d 0.0625$ | 0.0625 |\n\n**Končni seštevek (Final Sum):**\n$$ 16 + 8 + 0 + 0 + 1 + 0 + 0.25 + 0.125 + 0.0625 \u003d 25.4375 $$\nTherefore:\n$$ 11001.0111_2 \u003d 25.4375_{10} $$\n\n---\n\n## [[Primer: Uporaba Algoritemskega Pristopa]]\n\nLet\u0027s apply the 4-step method to the problem in your notes.\n\n### 1. [[Definicija Problema]]\n*   **Cilj (Goal):** Izračunati množino ($n$) in volumen ($V$) dušika ($N_2$).\n*   **Vhodni Podatki (Inputs):**\n    *   Masa dušika ($m$): $100 \\, g$\n    *   Temperatura ($T$): $23^\\circ C$\n    *   Tlak ($p$): $1.2 \\, atm$\n\n### 2. [[Analiza in Matematični Model]]\n*   **Formule (Formulas):**\n    1.  Množina snovi: $n \u003d \\frac{m}{M}$\n    2.  Plinska enačba idealnega plina: $pV \u003d nRT \\implies V \u003d \\frac{nRT}{p}$\n*   **Konstante (Constants):**\n    *   Molska masa dušika ($M_{N_2}$): $28 \\, g/mol$\n    *   Splošna plinska konstanta ($R$): $8.314 \\, \\frac{J}{mol \\cdot K}$\n\n*   **Pretvorba Enot (Unit Conversion):** This is a critical step. To use the gas constant $R$, all units must be in the SI system.\n    *   **Temperatura:**\n        $$ T(K) \u003d T(^\\circ C) + 273.15 \u003d 23 + 273.15 \u003d 296.15 \\, K $$\n    *   **Tlak:**\n        $$ 1 \\, \\text{atm} \u003d 101325 \\, \\text{Pa} $$\n        $$ p \u003d 1.2 \\, \\text{atm} \\times 101325 \\, \\frac{\\text{Pa}}{\\text{atm}} \u003d 121590 \\, \\text{Pa} $$\n\n### 3. [[Psevdokoda]]\nThis corresponds to the flowchart in your notes.\n\n```\n# Vhodni podatki\nm \u003d 100        # g\nT \u003d 296.15     # K\np \u003d 121590     # Pa\nM \u003d 28         # g/mol\nR \u003d 8.314      # J/(mol*K)\n\n# Izračuni\nn \u003d m / M\nV_m3 \u003d (n * R * T) / p\nV_L \u003d V_m3 * 1000\n\n# Izhod\nIzpiši \"Množina (mol):\", n\nIzpiši \"Volumen (L):\", V_L\n```\n\n### 4. [[Izračun in Rezultat]]\n1.  **Izračunaj množino (n):**\n    $$ n \u003d \\frac{100 \\, g}{28 \\, g/mol} \\approx 3.5714 \\, mol $$\n2.  **Izračunaj volumen v $m^3$ (V):**\n    $$ V \u003d \\frac{3.5714 \\, mol \\cdot 8.314 \\, \\frac{J}{mol \\cdot K} \\cdot 296.15 \\, K}{121590 \\, Pa} \\approx 0.07228 \\, m^3 $$\n3.  **Pretvori v Litre:**\n    $$ V_L \u003d 0.07228 \\, m^3 \\times 1000 \\, \\frac{L}{m^3} \\approx 72.28 \\, L $$",

        "text": " the result. The binary fraction is formed by reading these integer parts from **top to bottom**.\n\n**Primer (Example):** Convert the fraction"
      }, {
        "text": " `0.625` from $10.625_{10}$.\n\n| Množenje (Multi"
      }, {
        "text": "plication) | Rezultat (Result) | **Celi del (Integer Part)** |\n| :---: | :---: | :---"
      }, {
        "text": ": |\n| $0.625 \\times 2$ | 1.25 | **1"
      }, {
        "text": "** $\\downarrow$ (Read downwards) |\n| $0.25 \\times 2$ | 0.50 | **0**"
      }, {
        "text": " |\n| $0.50 \\times 2$ | 1.00 | **1** |\n\nThe"
      }, {
        "text": " process stops when the fractional part becomes 0. The fractional part is $0.625_{10} \u003d "
      }, {
        "text": "0.101_2$.\n\n**Končni Rezultat:** Combining both parts, we get:\n$$"
      }, {
        "text": " 10.625_{10} \u003d 1010.101_2 $$"
      }, {
        "text": "\n\n### [[Pretvarjanje: Dvojiški v Desetiški Sistem (Binary to Decimal)]]\n"
      }, {
        "text": "**Razlaga (The \"Why\"):** This conversion is a weighted sum of powers of 2. Each position in the binary"
      }, {
        "text": " number corresponds to a power of 2. The powers are positive for the integer part (starting from 0 at the decimal"
      }, {
        "text": " point and increasing to the left) and negative for the fractional part (starting from -1 and decreasing to the right).\n\n**Primer ("
      }, {
        "text": "Example):** Convert $11001.0111_2$ to decimal.\n\n| D"
      }, {
        "text": "vojiška Števka (Binary Digit) | Položaj ($i$) (Position) | Potenca ($"
      }, {
        "text": "2^i$) (Power) | Vrednost (Digit $\\times 2^i$) (Value) |\n| :---: |"
      }, {
        "text": " :---: | :---: | :---: |\n| 1 | 4 | $2^4 \u003d 16$"
      }, {
        "text": " | 16 |\n| 1 | 3 | $2^3 \u003d 8$ | 8"
      }, {
        "text": " |\n| 0 | 2 | $2^2 \u003d 4$ | 0 |\n| 0"
      }, {
        "text": " | 1 | $2^1 \u003d 2$ | 0 |\n| 1 | 0 | $2^0"
      }, {
        "text": " \u003d 1$ | 1 |\n| . | | (Decimal Point) | |\n| 0 | -"
      }, {
        "text": "1 | $2^{-1} \u003d 0.5$ | 0 |\n| 1 | -2 | $2^{-2} \u003d"
      }, {
        "text": " 0.25$ | 0.25 |\n| 1 | -3 | $2^{-"
      }, {
        "text": "3} \u003d 0.125$ | 0.125 |\n| 1 | -"
      }, {
        "text": "4 | $2^{-4} \u003d 0.0625$ | 0.0625"
      }, {
        "text": " |\n\n**Končni seštevek (Final Sum):**\n$$ 16 + 8 + 0"
      }, {
        "text": " + 0 + 1 + 0 + 0.25 + 0.125 + 0."
      }, {
        "text": "0625 \u003d 25.4375 $$\nTherefore:\n$$ 11001"
      }, {
        "text": ".0111_2 \u003d 25.4375_{10} $$\n\n---\n\n"
